#### A store listing that names the platform's company as developer is a maker statement; an admin section the platform calls part of its admin is built in
- **Decides:** two rulings after SH-2:
  - a store listing (Google Play, the App Store) naming the platform's company as an app's developer is a maker statement, whatever channel sells the app. Shopify Inbox is its own service on every line, from its publisher, Shopify Inc. It corrected the check after SH-54, which kept Inbox built in;
  - an admin section the platform's own words call part of its admin is built in, whatever its route's permission. Agentic Storefronts is the case: 'Agentic Storefronts now has its own page in your Shopify admin'. A held statement outranks B-extent's convention that a route gated by the apps permission is an app's page.
- **Why:** B-extent's own service names the platform's company as maker; a statement outranks a convention ([rulings, lines 126 to 127](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L126-L127)).
- **Risks:** about 15 lines name the first and about 4 the second; 8 and 2 lines were aligned. Built in and its own service both count, so counts move only where the line was left to apps before (a reading).
- **Instead:** a maker statement only in the platform's own posts; and B-extent's permission convention over the post. Agentic's routes would be left to apps.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 126 to 127](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L126-L127)

#### A Shopify-branded app is read by the kind of entry that names it
- **Decides:** a page at the app's own address with no maker stated is shown only on what the app does, and built in on CM-APP-007 (M-10). A post in which 'we' make, upgrade or ship the app makes its things its own service. Its middle case, a post or card naming the app with no maker 'built in by what it shows', was replaced by the ruling after SH-43. (the ruling after SH-36, in part)
- **Why:** each kind of entry states a different thing about the app ([rulings, lines 112 to 115](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L112-L115)).
- **Risks:** 39 lines name it. Its in-force parts follow M-10 and the ruling after SH-35.
- **Instead:** one rule for every kind of entry.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 112 to 115](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L112-L115)

#### Shop, Shopify's consumer app, does its things as its own service
- **Decides:** Shop (SH-S-0056) does its things as its own service: carts synced across a shopper's devices, one-tap sign-in, cart reminders. (the ruling after SH-34, the second of three)
- **Why:** as Salla's own apps do (the ruling after SA-17) ([rulings, line 104](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L104)).
- **Risks:** about 67 lines name the ruling with Shop's name. Both supports count, so few counts move.
- **Instead:** built in where Shop's feature works in the store's own checkout. Shop Pay in checkout is already read so ([rulings, line 169](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L169)).
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 104](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L104)

#### A third party's own feature through the platform's interface is left to apps, beside the provider's line
- **Decides:** a third party's own feature that the platform's interface carries, such as Avalara's return filing, is left to apps on the capability whose thing it is (CM-TAX-006), beside the provider's own line. (M-8)
- **Why:** the third party does the thing through what the platform provides ([rulings, line 14](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L14)).
- **Risks:** 92 lines name it. If the platform does the work and the third party only supplies data, those cells are slots where they should count.
- **Instead:** one line, on the provider's capability only. Fewer cells would be held.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 14](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L14)

#### A first-party app's feature takes another capability's line only where the app itself provides that thing
- **Decides:** Stocky's transfers are stock transfers, so they stand on CM-INV-006. Shopify Collective's supplier shipping rates, its inventory confirmed between stores and its price lists stay in Collective's own CM-CHN-005 line. SH-57's four lines on CM-SHP-001, CM-INV-001 and CM-B2B-003 were dropped. (the ruling after SH-57)
- **Why:** Collective's own thing is selling and sourcing between the platform's stores; the rates are a means of it ([rulings, line 140](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L140)).
- **Risks:** 26 lines name it; 4 lines dropped and 2 more after SH-39 ([rulings, line 158](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L158)). If Collective's rates are shipping rates, CM-SHP-001 and the others lose evidence.
- **Instead:** a line on each capability whose thing the feature does.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 140](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L140)

#### A tooltip's 'you can build X' is shown only; what a plan line includes is built in
- **Decides:** a statement that makes the merchant responsible ('you can build a loyalty program with it') is shown only, never outside. What a plan line 'includes' is built in. (M-9)
- **Why:** the merchant does it, so the platform doesn't ([rulings, line 13](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L13)).
- **Risks:** 15 lines name it. If the platform's tools do the work, those cells undercount.
- **Instead:** built in, the platform's tools being the means.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 13](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L13)

#### A provider's role is placed only from the entry and its sources
- **Decides:** a provider's role known only from another platform's cell, such as instalments or cryptocurrency, is a lead for the second stage, not evidence. (M-5)
- **Why:** each platform's cells rest on its own evidence ([rulings, line 11](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L11)).
- **Risks:** 4 lines name it. A cell may stay empty until the second stage reads it.
- **Instead:** carry the role over from the other platform, marked a reading. Cells would fill sooner, on another platform's word.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 11](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L11)

#### A services marketplace's category is CM-PTN-005's, not the app marketplace's
- **Decides:** a category of the platform's marketplace of services, such as Salla's Merchant Services or Shopify's Partner Directory, is left to apps on CM-PTN-005 (partners' services). It is shown only on the capability whose work it names. The four cells SA-25's categories would have made left to apps (CM-CAT-001, CM-CNT-008, CM-MKT-010, CM-SFR-002) keep their placement. (the ruling after SA-25)
- **Why:** B-extent's marketplace category is apps, themes or extensions plugged into the platform. An expert does a person's service, with the platform's own tools ([rulings, lines 74 to 77](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L74-L77)).
- **Risks:** about 15 lines name it. It kept four cells from moving.
- **Instead:** a services category is left to apps on its work's capability. The four cells would move to left to apps.
- **Weight:** medium: four cells' support.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 74 to 77](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L74-L77)

#### A feature listed for a partner's account takes the support of the page it gates
- **Decides:** a feature in a partner's account gates the partner's own tools, so the tool's thing sets the support: the expert marketplace left to apps, Salla's own billing built in. 'A released flag supports built in' stays for a store's features over the platform's capabilities. (the ruling after SA-44, the first of two)
- **Why:** as the routes placed them ([rulings, lines 86 to 90](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L86-L90)).
- **Risks:** a feature line changes nothing the routes didn't ([rulings, line 90](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L90)).
- **Instead:** every released partner feature built in, as a store's is.
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 86 to 90](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L86-L90)

#### A route that only redirects is shown only on its target's capability
- **Decides:** a route whose page is another's, by a redirect, is shown only on its target's capability, and the target takes its own line. The concern map's built in on Shopify's `balance:inactive:redirect-statements` (SH-S-5044) is read again at the amendment. Salla's settings `s:redirect` follows the same rule, which corrected SA-48's S8, where it was no thing of the class. (the rulings after SH-8 and SA-49)
- **Why:** the page is the target's ([rulings, line 100](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L100); [rulings, line 108](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L108)).
- **Risks:** about 82 lines name the ruling after SH-8 with a redirect. If a redirect is a real entry point of its own, those lines undercount.
- **Instead:** built in, as the platform's own route. More lines would be built in, on cells the target already holds.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 100](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L100); [rulings, line 108](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L108)

#### A route's intent that the platform documents for apps takes a left-to-apps line
- **Decides:** where Shopify's Intents API documents an intent, by which an app opens the admin at a route, the route takes a left-to-apps line on its capability. An intent no held document describes is named in the reading. (the ruling after SH-8, the third of three)
- **Why:** an app opens the admin there ([rulings, line 102](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L102)).
- **Risks:** about 67 lines name the ruling with an intent. Left to apps doesn't count, so it adds no count, and the built-in line stands beside it.
- **Instead:** no line for the intent, the route's own built-in line alone.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 102](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L102)

#### Shopify Capital's own features are its own service, and Shopify's POS is built in
- **Decides:** two amendment rulings:
  - Shopify Capital's maker is held (SH-S-0484, SH-S-0491, SH-S-0493), so its own features are its own service, and its admin section and application built in. The Editions' 10 lines that had it shown only, by the brand alone (the Editions digest's rule 21), became its own service, and 2 stay built in;
  - CM-POS-001's its own service, which rests on the POS app's listing (SH-S-2757), is read again: the POS is the plans' own channel, built in, by the ruling after SH-55.
- **Why:** the router digest's table holds Capital's maker statements ([amendment notes, line 11](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/amendment-notes.md?plain=1#L11)); the POS is a sales channel the plans include ([amendment notes, line 25](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/amendment-notes.md?plain=1#L25)).
- **Risks:** shown only doesn't count and its own service does, so Capital's 10 lines move counts where they hold a cell alone (a reading).
- **Instead:** keep Capital shown only where the card names it by its brand alone.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [amendment notes, line 11](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/amendment-notes.md?plain=1#L11); [amendment notes, line 25](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/amendment-notes.md?plain=1#L25); [align-no-maker.md, lines 81 to 104](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/align-no-maker.md?plain=1#L81-L104)

#### The alignment's own decisions on Shopify's lines with no maker held
Shared risk: each decides one line or a few, on one app's maker. Shared alternative: leave the line as its batch placed it, for the final review.

| Decision | Decides | Why | Weight | Source |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| POS's quick count extension stays built in | no held words call it an app, so it is POS's own | as the Pixel Helper is the admin's | low | [align-no-maker.md, line 40](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/align-no-maker.md?plain=1#L40) |
| ShipSurance's insurance stays built in | it comes with labels bought in the admin, claimed in the admin, as Shopify Shipping insurance is; ShipSurance's role a gap | the admin's words | low | [align-no-maker.md, line 51](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/align-no-maker.md?plain=1#L51) |
| Shop Quiz stays left to apps | the card names it as the quizzes' source, as it names Tolstoy, Videowise and Showday. It alone makes CM-MKT-023 held on Shopify | E124 | medium: it holds a cell | [align-no-maker.md, line 111](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/align-no-maker.md?plain=1#L111); [align-no-maker.md, line 165](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/align-no-maker.md?plain=1#L165) |
| Bill Pay's 'none held' | read as no maker statement naming Shopify; Melio is held as its provider, outside, in CM-PAY-027's cell | SH-S-7200 | low | [align-no-maker.md, line 121](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/align-no-maker.md?plain=1#L121); [align-no-maker.md, line 165](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/align-no-maker.md?plain=1#L165) |
| TikTok's and Roku's apps: the venue | shown only, the venue its filler (SH-55:111, SH-50:210, SH-52:33). SH-42:209 keeps left to apps, as it names Roku Ad Manager, Roku's own service; Faire's own wholesale selling keeps left to apps | C46.3's split | medium: 3 lines | [align-no-maker.md, line 164](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/align-no-maker.md?plain=1#L164) |
| Forms' and Order Printer's missed lines | three more lines to shown only, by the ruling after SH-43 | no maker held | low | [align-no-maker.md, line 164](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/align-no-maker.md?plain=1#L164) |

#### The digests' rules that decide a support of their own
Each rule was made from its batch's cases and kept by the coordinator at that batch's check. None had an independent review.
- **Shared risk:** where a rule is wrong, every line of its kind in the structure has the wrong support. It moves a count only where those lines hold a cell alone.
- **Shared alternative:** the general rulings above alone, each case read again at the final review.
- **Weight:** medium unless the row says otherwise.

| changelog, [changelog digest, line 167](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L167) (C44.2) | a page that a new action appears on takes a built-in line | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 168](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L168) (C44.3) | a time-limited initiative the platform runs itself is built in while it lasts | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 171](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L171) (C44.6) | identity providers behind the platform's sign-in are left to apps; 'first-party integrations' names a means, not a separate product | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 185](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L185) (C45.8) | draft orders that Shopify Ping and Shopify Chat send in a conversation are CM-ORD-005 its own service | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 190](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L190) (C46.1) | the Shopify Plus wholesale channel is a sales channel the Plus plan includes, built in | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 192](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L192) (C46.3) | a channel named for a third party, with no maker held (Pinterest, Instagram), is shown only for the channel and left to apps for the third party's own feature, the third party the venue | it set the alignment's TikTok and Roku lines |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 194](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L194) (C46.5) | a retired app's maker statement doesn't pass to the app that replaces it | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 111](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L111) (Customer View) | the Customer View app is built in, part of POS, not a separate app | low |

### 2. What is one capability: a place of its own, one thing in one domain, joins, nesting, homonyms

#### A container goes on the capability its title names, or becomes a new thing
- **Decides:** a container, such as a sidebar group, a section of sections or a menu's parent, is never no thing of the class. It goes on the capability its title or group names. Failing that, it goes on the capability whose page its own path opens. Failing both, it is `new:<its title>`, built in, as the platform's own admin place, for the coordinator to decide with the other new things. It takes a second line where its own page holds another thing. Limited since: a container over the whole admin, over several sections with no title of its own, or whose title names only a grouping, is no thing of the class (group 4). (M-13, the join's D3)
- **Why:** the platform's own admin place is something it builds; its title says what it holds ([rulings, line 28](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L28)).
- **Risks:** 451 lines name it: Salla 187, Shopify 171, WooCommerce 93. It made many of the new things the coordinator decided. If a container is only navigation, those lines add support to cells that their children already hold, and some new things are no thing of the class.
- **Instead:** a container gives no line of its own, its children placed alone. Fewer lines and fewer new things.
- **Weight:** high: 451 lines, and new capabilities.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 28](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L28)

#### A thing an entry names counts in each capability it names; a child takes its parent's line only where it is a kind of the parent's thing
- **Decides:** an entry that names several things takes a line on each. A child's entry takes its parent's line too where it is a kind of the parent's thing, as Bank deposit, under OFFLINE PAYMENT METHODS, is an offline payment method. A group the entry only sits under, whose title says where it is and not what it is (BigCommerce's `group: General`), gives it no line. (M-6, refined after the calibration)
- **Why:** domain C's rule, "a statement that names several things counts in each" ([rulings, line 18](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L18)). Read as every section's group, the first wording gave 7 BigCommerce lines at `high` and 14 at `xhigh` that changed no cell ([rulings, line 19](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L19)).
- **Risks:** 425 lines name it, 340 of them Shopify's. If 'a kind of' is read too widely, parents gain support their children don't show; too narrowly, a parent cell holding nothing stays empty.
- **Instead:** the first wording, every section's group, which adds lines that change no cell. Or no parent line at all, which leaves some parents empty.
- **Weight:** high: 425 lines.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 18 to 19](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L18-L19)

#### Each staff permission is built in on the capability it gates
- **Decides:** in a list of permissions, such as Zid's `store-permissions` or Shopify's role permissions, each permission is built in on the capability it gates. Only a permission over the staff's permissions themselves goes on CM-STF-002 (staff roles). (the ruling after ZD-7; the brief's ruling)
- **Why:** CM-STF-002's note keeps them so ([rulings, line 48](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L48); [H-held.md, line 129](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/H-held.md?plain=1#L129)).
- **Risks:** 444 lines name it: Shopify 263, Salla 181. A permission can make a cell that held nothing held as built in, which moves its count. If a permission only shows the platform guards a thing, and not that it does it, those cells overcount (a reading). ZD-8, a list of permissions, changed no cell ([method report, line 317](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L317)).
- **Instead:** every permission on CM-STF-002 alone, as evidence of staff roles. Some gated cells would hold nothing again.
- **Weight:** high: 444 lines.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 48](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L48); [H-held.md, line 129](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/H-held.md?plain=1#L129)

#### Things a page names only in a list take no line; a post's own thing takes its line
- **Decides:** a list of what may be used or changed names things in passing, and they take no line. The page's own thing does. Example: the ready stores' FAQ answer 'customize everything such as plan, theme design, settings, products' is placed on CM-DVT-005 alone. A statement about one of those things is placed on it. For changelog posts, Shopify's brand on a product named in passing is no maker statement. (the ruling after SA-41, and its use for posts)
- **Why:** the things a page lists in passing are not a second thing of the page ([rulings, line 98](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L98); [rulings, line 106](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L106)).
- **Risks:** 128 lines name it, 111 of them Shopify's. A cell holding nothing whose only statement is in such a list stays empty.
- **Instead:** a line for each thing named, shown only. More cells would hold a statement, none counted.
- **Weight:** high: 128 lines.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 98](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L98); [rulings, line 106](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L106)

#### BigCommerce's Extensions aren't the platform's own apps
- **Decides:** BigCommerce's terms define an Extension as "a service developed by a third party but implemented and provided by Commerce" ([rulings, line 29](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L29)). That isn't CM-APP-011, apps the platform's company makes. The five lines take `new:Extensions`, shown only, no place of its own. The model's own CM-APP-011 BigCommerce cell rested on the same homonym: its own service became shown only, and CM-APP-011's count drops by one platform. Extensions was then decided no thing of the class, its rows staying on CM-ACC-002 ([new.tsv, line 5](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new.tsv?plain=1#L5)). (M-2)
- **Why:** the word means another thing on BigCommerce ([rulings, line 29](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L29)).
- **Risks:** it moved CM-APP-011's count by one. If 'provided by Commerce' makes them BigCommerce's own service, the count is one too low.
- **Instead:** keep Extensions on CM-APP-011 as its own service, read by 'provided by Commerce'.
- **Weight:** high: it moves a count.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 29](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L29); [new.tsv, line 5](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new.tsv?plain=1#L5)

#### A store's llms.txt is a file for search, not a channel for buyers' AI
- **Decides:** a store's llms.txt is built in on CM-CNT-007 (SEO) where the platform writes it. It is shown only on CM-AIA-005 (buyers discovering and buying through AI chats and agents) where its words name AI platforms. It corrected three Zid lines, ZD-1's built in on CM-AIA-005 among them. Zid's and Salla's CM-AIA-005 cells are then held with no placement. (the ruling after SA-2)
- **Why:** the file gives buyers no channel and no purchase, and both platforms keep it in their SEO pages ([rulings, lines 58 to 63](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L58-L63)).
- **Risks:** 8 lines name it. It took Zid's count off CM-AIA-005. If llms.txt is the platform's way into AI chats, CM-AIA-005 undercounts two platforms.
- **Instead:** built in on CM-AIA-005 too, where it names AI platforms. Zid and Salla would count there.
- **Weight:** high: it moves a count.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 58 to 63](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L58-L63)

#### A second line follows the page, not the link's words
- **Decides:** where an address's page is itself two things, every link to it takes both lines, in either language. The Arabic link أدوات التتبع ('tracking tools') opens Pixels & feeds, so it takes both of that page's lines, CM-MKT-009 and CM-CHN-004. A line only one language names is added; none is taken away. (the ruling after SA-19)
- **Why:** the link opens the page, so it is the page ([rulings, line 99](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L99)).
- **Risks:** about 70 lines name it, 54 of them Shopify's. Lines on cells the page already holds.
- **Instead:** a link takes only the line its own words name.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 99](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L99)

#### A second line on an address is only for a page that is itself two things
- **Decides:** a line that would rest only on a gate's feature goes in the reading, not on its own line. This settled SA-21's rule for SA-23. (the ruling after SA-22, the second)
- **Why:** as M-6 refined has it: a gate is a condition, not a thing of the page ([rulings, line 84](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L84)).
- **Risks:** about 17 lines name it with these words. A gate's feature that is a capability of its own gets no line from the gated page.
- **Instead:** a line for the gate's feature, shown only.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 84](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L84)

#### A first-party app's post is about the app whose page its link opens
- **Decides:** where a post's words name no other app, it is about the app whose page its own link opens. 'Combine repeat bundle options' links the Shopify Bundles page, so it is that app's update, and its own service, by two held posts naming its maker. SH-35's two lines and SH-37's one were aligned. (the ruling after SH-38, the second)
- **Why:** the link shows which app is meant ([rulings, line 125](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L125)).
- **Risks:** about 17 lines name the ruling. A link to a help page about an app may not make the post the app's.
- **Instead:** read only the post's words; a post that names no app is the platform's.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 125](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L125)

#### A card in a page's two languages is one thing
- **Decides:** where the English and Arabic lines are one element, by their place or the layer the English one is named for, they take the capability of the words that name the thing more exactly. 'Run discount promotions' is 'إنشاء كوبونات تخفيض', coupons (CM-MKT-002). A thing only one language names takes its own line, since either language is the platform's statement. (the ruling after SA-4)
- **Why:** both languages state the same card ([rulings, lines 69 to 73](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L69-L73)).
- **Risks:** 31 lines name it, all Salla's. If the two languages state different things, a capability loses a line.
- **Instead:** each language's line placed alone.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 69 to 73](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L69-L73)

#### The platform's changelog is CM-ONB-003
- **Decides:** the platform's changelog is CM-ONB-003, the platform's news and learning for its merchants. A category naming only a grouping of posts (تحسينات المنصة) goes there too. A category or post that names a feature goes on the feature's capability. This is a reading. (the ruling after SA-2 and SA-5, the second)
- **Why:** salla.com's footer lists its updates centre under الموارد with the help centre, academy and community ([rulings, lines 55 to 57](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L55-L57)).
- **Risks:** about 19 lines name the ruling with the changelog. If a changelog is the platform's developer or status record, CM-ONB-003 holds the wrong evidence.
- **Instead:** a capability of its own for the platform's dated record of changes. Q3's miss M-09 asks for one ([S2-held-design.md, line 260](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L260)).
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 55 to 57](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L55-L57)

#### An export's column or an importer's field is a field the platform keeps on its records
- **Decides:** it is built in on the capability whose data it holds. An order's Order Source (`utm_source`) is CM-MKT-008's; a product's Cost Price (`cost_price`) is CM-CAT-012's. It corrected SA-25's rule E2, which made such a column shown only. (the ruling after SA-25 and SA-26)
- **Why:** WooCommerce's Order Attribution and Cost of Goods Sold keep the same fields ([rulings, lines 78 to 81](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L78-L81)).
- **Risks:** about 26 lines name it with these words. A column can be a field no feature uses. Then it counts a capability the platform only stores data for.
- **Instead:** SA-25's shown only, on the capability the column's title names.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 78 to 81](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L78-L81)

#### The admin's own interface language is one new thing across the platforms
- **Decides:** a dashboard's or portal's language menu, or an admin language setting, is one new thing across the platforms: Salla's `العربية / English`, and Shopify's Admin languages. It became CM-SRF-003. (the ruling after SA-49, the third)
- **Why:** it bears on a market, and no capability held it ([rulings, line 109](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L109); [new-proposals.md, lines 31 to 36](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.md?plain=1#L31-L36)).
- **Risks:** 6 lines name it. If the language is part of the account's profile, a capability exists that should be CM-ACC-001's child.
- **Instead:** CM-ACC-001's child in domain A, since Salla puts it in Your profile ([new-proposals.md, line 35](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.md?plain=1#L35)).
- **Weight:** medium: it made a capability.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 109](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L109)

#### A mark is never a quality
- **Decides:** a plan's mark of inclusion (a check, 'Available', 'Included') is the line's scope. A provider's feature mark (Recurring Payments ✔) is a statement of the marked feature's capability. It takes a line there, shown only unless the row says who does it, the provider its filler. Settled within B-extent; the rulings restate it. (D4)
- **Why:** a mark says the thing is there, not how much ([rulings, lines 20 to 22](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L20-L22)).
- **Risks:** no line names D4. The provider marks add shown-only lines to the marked features' cells.
- **Instead:** a provider's mark is a quality of the provider's line, and the feature's cell holds nothing.
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 20 to 22](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L20-L22)

#### A registry of the platform's own apps is a container of their routes
- **Decides:** in a registry such as Salla's `portal-apps`, which says which app serves which addresses, an app's entry takes one line, on the capability its title or main page names. Its routes carry the rest. SA-39's lines, which give each app a line on every capability its routes were placed on, stand: right, only many. (the ruling after SA-39)
- **Why:** the routes are placed by their own entries ([rulings, line 92](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L92)).
- **Risks:** none on the cells: SA-39's lines stand.
- **Instead:** SA-39's way, a line per capability.
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 92](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L92)

#### The new things: a capability of their own, onto an existing one, or no thing of the class
The held pass named things no capability held: `new:<name>`. An agent proposed a decision for each across the five platforms ([new-proposals.md, lines 13 to 18](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.md?plain=1#L13-L18)). The coordinator decided them in `held/new.tsv`.
- **The decisions:** a new capability where at least one platform gives the thing a place of its own and no capability's definition or cells hold it. Onto an existing capability where its definition holds the thing, or, for a thing with no place of its own, onto the nearest capability. No thing of the class where it has no place of its own and is no thing of the class, or is a title naming only a grouping, or is the admin's frame.
- **The counts, from the files:** of the 84 proposed things, 29 make 16 new capabilities, 39 go onto existing capabilities and 16 are no thing of the class. Seven things more, BigCommerce's five and Zid's two, were decided on 30 October. `STATE.md` and the decision's commit had said "52 onto existing ones" ([STATE.md, line 259](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/STATE.md?plain=1#L259)). By `new.tsv` it is 39, with the 29 things in the 16 new capabilities: the record had counted each new capability as one thing. `STATE.md` is corrected.
- **Shared risk:**
  - a new capability that is really part of an existing one gives the class one capability too many, with a count of its own;
  - a thing put onto an existing capability that has a place of its own leaves the class a capability short;
  - a thing ruled no thing of the class loses its lines from the model.
- **Shared alternative:** the other two decisions.
- **Weight:**
  - each new capability is high, since it adds a capability with its count;
  - onto an existing capability is medium, since it adds evidence to a cell and can make a cell that held nothing held;
  - no thing of the class is low, unless the row says otherwise.
- **Lines:** each thing's lines are from `held/new-proposals.tsv`.

#### Timeline events is a capability of its own (N20, changed from the proposal)
- **Decides:** Shopify's API groups Timeline events (GraphQL) and Events (REST) are a new capability, record timelines, the events on the store's records: CM-OPS-007, made in G-amend with each platform's cells.
- **Why:** the proposal put them onto CM-ORD-013 (notes and comments on an order), widened to 'on an order and the store's other records' ([new-proposals.tsv, line 34](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.tsv?plain=1#L34)). The coordinator: CM-ORD-013 holds notes and comments on an order, while the API groups record events on the store's records, a place of its own ([new.tsv, line 41](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new.tsv?plain=1#L41)).
- **Risks:** 2 lines. If timelines are an order's notes, the class has one capability too many, a count resting on API groups alone.
- **Instead:** the proposal: CM-ORD-013 widened.
- **Weight:** high: a capability.
- **Source:** [new.tsv, line 41](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new.tsv?plain=1#L41); [new-proposals.tsv, line 34](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.tsv?plain=1#L34)

#### Sandbox organizations go onto the partner's test stores (N13, changed from the proposal)
- **Decides:** Shopify's Sandbox organizations, two Partner Dashboard pages, go onto CM-DVT-003, widened to 'the stores and organizations a partner keeps'.
- **Why:** the proposal made them a new capability under CM-DVT-003 ([new-proposals.tsv, line 27](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.tsv?plain=1#L27)). The coordinator: a sandbox organization is a test organization a partner keeps in its dashboard, as its test stores are. What one holds isn't stated, a reading ([new.tsv, line 34](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new.tsv?plain=1#L34)).
- **Risks:** 4 lines, all built in. Both pages answered 'not found' for the held partner, so what the thing is rests on its modules' names.
- **Instead:** the proposal: a capability of its own, CM-DVT-007.
- **Weight:** medium: a widened definition, no new count.
- **Source:** [new.tsv, line 34](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new.tsv?plain=1#L34); [new-proposals.tsv, line 27](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.tsv?plain=1#L27)

#### Gifting a Salla plan is buying the platform's plan (N17, changed from the proposal)
- **Decides:** Salla's Gift a Salla plan (أهدِ من تحب باقة سلة), a settings item linking `giftcards.resal.me`, goes onto CM-PLN-001, the platform's plans.
- **Why:** the proposal made it a new capability, gifting a plan, CM-PLN-009 ([new-proposals.tsv, line 31](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.tsv?plain=1#L31)). The coordinator: gifting a plan is a way to buy the platform's plan for another store. The gift card's terms, not held, would say whether a third party sells it ([new.tsv, line 38](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new.tsv?plain=1#L38)).
- **Risks:** 1 line, built in. If a third party sells the gift card, the line is outside, not the platform's plan.
- **Instead:** the proposal's own capability; or CM-PLN-005 (paying for a plan), if the gift card is a means of paying.
- **Weight:** low: one line.
- **Source:** [new.tsv, line 38](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new.tsv?plain=1#L38); [new-proposals.tsv, line 31](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.tsv?plain=1#L31)

#### Bug Bounty is a capability (N12)
- **Decides:** Shopify's Bug Bounty, a Partner Dashboard route, is a new capability: the platform's security research programme, CM-TRU-022, in domain A with no parent. The platform rewards security researchers who report vulnerabilities in its own software ([A-amend capabilities, line 6](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/parts/A-amend/capabilities.tsv?plain=1#L6)).
- **Why:** 'Shopify's Bug Bounty program is our way to reward security researchers…' (SH-86's notes), a trust thing of the platform itself, beside its own policies ([A-amend capabilities, line 6](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/parts/A-amend/capabilities.tsv?plain=1#L6)).
- **Risks:** 2 lines. The decision's commit and `STATE.md` had listed it among four proposals the coordinator changed ([STATE.md, line 259](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/STATE.md?plain=1#L259)). Yet `new-proposals.tsv` already proposed a new capability ([new-proposals.tsv, line 26](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.tsv?plain=1#L26)), so nothing changed: the coordinator changed three proposals and kept this one, and `STATE.md` is corrected. A merchant doesn't use a bug bounty on a store. A later ruling makes a tool of the platform's company that no merchant or app uses on a store no thing of the class ([Editions digest, line 255](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/editions-page-data.md?plain=1#L255)). The coordinator doesn't apply it here, a reading: that ruling's cases are the company's tools apart from any store (an open-source pipeline tool, a phone keyboard), while a bounty on the platform's own software guards the stores that run on it, as its card-data compliance does (CM-TRU-018, built in). If that reading is wrong, the class has a capability it shouldn't.
- **Instead:** no thing of the class, by that later ruling.
- **Weight:** high: a capability with its count.
- **Source:** [new.tsv, line 33](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new.tsv?plain=1#L33); [new-proposals.tsv, line 26](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/new-proposals.tsv?plain=1#L26)

#### The digests' rules that decide a capability of their own
- **Shared risk:** the lines of each rule's kind sit on the wrong capability, leaving another cell short, or holding nothing where it should hold something.
- **Shared alternative:** the capability the entry's own words name first, settled at the final review.
- **Weight:** medium unless the row says otherwise.

| changelog, [changelog digest, line 183](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L183) (C45.6) | the platform's status page is CM-OPS-002 | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 184](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L184) (C45.7) | tax on Shopify's own bills is CM-PLN-007 | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 191](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L191) (C46.2) | an app's own interface languages take `new:Admin languages`, since CM-SRF-003 | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 193](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L193) (C46.4) | 3D Secure goes on CM-PAY-002, and on CM-PAY-009 for its effect on chargebacks; Shopify's 3D Secure is since CM-PAY-033 (N11) | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 195](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L195) (after SH-46) | the admin's own tools for advertising on ad platforms are CM-MKT-010 built in | |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 198](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L198) (C47) | a carrier of the platform's own labels newly offered is CM-SHP-002 built in and CM-SHP-015 shown only; 8 lines aligned | |

### 3. Scope and conditions: plans, markets, gates

#### Only a route's own gates on the store are its scope; an ancestor's are named in its reading, by script
- **Decides:** a route's scope is its own gates on the store: features, entitlements, regions, plans, the platform's hashed betas. An ancestor's gate belongs to the ancestor. The coordinator names it in the reading of every line under it, by script (`method/held_router_gates.py`). The agent doesn't look up ancestors. A country named in a route's path goes in the reading and terms, not the scope (the router digest's R18.3). (the ruling after SH-8, the second of three)
- **Why:** a route manifest's eligibility is the route's own ([rulings, line 101](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L101); [rulings, line 107](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L107)).
- **Risks:** 479 lines' readings carry an ancestor's gate added by the script. A line reached only under a gated ancestor shows a general scope. Read alone, its scope looks wider than it is (a reading). The router digest's rule 28 keeps one case: a route under an ancestor whose beta is false in both held stores is shown only ([router digest, line 46](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/admin-router.md?plain=1#L46)).
- **Instead:** each line's scope joins its ancestors' gates with ` and `, as the settings tree does (below).
- **Weight:** high: 479 lines.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 101](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L101)

#### A line takes its own scope where its thing is stated under its own condition
- **Decides:** an entry's lines share one scope, except where a thing is stated under its own condition. A disclaimer's bank named for one market (WebBank, the United States), or an FAQ answer named for one plan (Plus), take that condition on their own line, and the entry's other lines keep theirs. (the ruling after SH-69, extending the brief's rule)
- **Why:** the condition belongs to the thing it is stated for ([rulings, line 116](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L116)).
- **Risks:** 137 lines name it, 134 of them Shopify's. Wrong, a condition would narrow or widen the wrong line.
- **Instead:** the brief's first rule: one scope per entry, the narrower conditions in the reading.
- **Weight:** high: 137 lines.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 116](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L116)

#### Flags, badges and release states
These decide whether a thing the platform holds back from some stores counts as built in. Lines' readings name 'early access' 104 times and `released: false` or `is_experimental` 54 times.
- **Shared risk:** a thing still in preview counted as built in raises a count early. A thing released counted as shown only lowers it.
- **Shared alternative:** every flag the platform ships is built in, its state in the scope; or every flag short of general release is shown only.
- **Weight:** high for the rules together, by their lines; each row medium.

| Ruling | Decides | Source |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Not released | a flag the source states isn't released (`released: false`, `is_experimental: true`) is shown only, whatever its value for the store | [H-held.md, line 119](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/H-held.md?plain=1#L119) |
| Released | a released flag is built in, its plans the scope, or the store's label where it names no plan; its value for the store goes in the reading | [H-held.md, line 120](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/H-held.md?plain=1#L120) |
| Off by default, no toggle (the ruling after WC-6) | a released flag the platform leaves off by default and hides from the admin (WooCommerce's `disable_ui` true with `enabled_by_default` false) isn't offered to merchants: shown only. Order Fulfillments and Cart & Checkout Blocks are defined so. 25 lines name `disable_ui` | [rulings, line 50](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L50); [H-held.md, line 121](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/H-held.md?plain=1#L121) |
| A store's flag by its value | Zid's `feature_flags`: `true` is built in, the store the scope; `false` is shown only; a pilot, preview or test name (`is_mazeed_pilot`) is shown only while `false` | [H-held.md, line 122](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/H-held.md?plain=1#L122) |
| Early access, previews | what the platform states is in early access, a preview or a beta for only some merchants is shown only, its words in the reading | [H-held.md, line 123](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/H-held.md?plain=1#L123) |
| Shopify's hashed betas | a route gated only by a hashed beta is built in where the beta is on in a held store, that store the scope; shown only where it is off in every held store | [H-held.md, line 124](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/H-held.md?plain=1#L124) |
| A release badge (D1) | a page listed with a badge, `new` or `beta`, with no limit stated, is offered to the store: built in, the badge in the reading. It corrected the brief, which had read 'a beta' among flags not released. 47 lines name D1 | [rulings, line 27](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L27); [H-held.md, line 125](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/H-held.md?plain=1#L125) |
| A flag named for a third party | it is the capability's evidence, never a filler | [H-held.md, line 126](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/H-held.md?plain=1#L126) |
| Before release, by the merchant (the ruling after SH-34, the first) | a feature the merchant can switch on before its release is built in, its plans the scope; the preview and its date in the reading. Shopify's Feature test drives (switch-on previews) are the case. About 14 lines name it so | [rulings, line 103](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L103) |

#### An entry may take a second line on one capability where it names another thing of it under its own condition
- **Decides:** a post's payment methods, each in its own countries, each take a line on the capability, with its own scope and filler. The checker allows it. (the ruling after SH-34, the third)
- **Why:** each method is stated under its own condition ([rulings, line 105](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L105)).
- **Risks:** about 24 lines name it so. More lines on one cell; no count moves.
- **Instead:** one line, the conditions joined in its scope.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 105](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L105)

#### A paid add-on's offer takes its site as scope; what comes with it takes the add-on
- **Decides:** the POS Pro card, the offer with its price, takes only its site as scope. A feature that comes with POS Pro takes 'POS Pro' as its scope, beside its site. (the ruling after SH-70)
- **Why:** the offer is open to any store on the site; its features only to those who buy it ([rulings, line 117](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L117)).
- **Risks:** 97 lines name it, all Shopify's. A feature scoped to an add-on reads as a narrower offer than one in every plan.
- **Instead:** the add-on as the scope of its own line too.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 117](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L117)

#### In a scope, `;` separates alternatives and ` and ` adds a condition that must also hold
- **Decides:** ` and ` takes the whole list before it: `team;special;pro and bullet-delivery-v2` is one of those plans with that feature. A scope that only lists needs no ` and `. Nested gates (a section's feature with a node's own, a gate with a market inside it) are joined with ` and `. SA-50 to SA-53's 23 such scopes, written with `;`, were rewritten. ` and ` joins two lists, each read as it is alone. A source's own words holding 'and' ('Basic plans and above') are quoted, so they aren't read as the join; nine lines were quoted. (the rulings after SA-53, SA-56 and SH-38)
- **Why:** one notation on every platform, as the router's gates are written ([rulings, line 118](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L118); [rulings, lines 123 to 124](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L123-L124)).
- **Risks:** 48 lines name the ruling after SA-53. A scope misread joins or splits conditions. This is wording, but a reader of the cell would read the condition wrongly.
- **Instead:** a structured scope (a list of conditions) in place of a string.
- **Weight:** low: notation.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 118](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L118); [rulings, lines 123 to 124](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L123-L124)

#### In a settings tree, a gate node's plans or features are the scope of every node inside it
- **Decides:** an `s:plan-gate`, a gated list, or a section gated by a feature (Wallet's `customer-wallet`, Loyalty's `loyalty-system`) is a gate node. Its condition is the scope of every node inside it, and a node's own gate is added with ` and `. An `if` on the store's feature or plan is a gate the same way. An `if` on the store's own data or a form's value goes in the reading. Unlike a route manifest, where an ancestor's gate is only named. (the rulings after SA-49 and SA-50)
- **Why:** the gate node's own thing is the gate over its content ([rulings, line 107](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L107); [rulings, line 110](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L110)).
- **Risks:** about 38 and 17 lines name them, almost all Salla's.
- **Instead:** as the router: each node's own gate alone, the ancestor's named.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 107](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L107); [rulings, line 110](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L110)

#### An offer the platform lists but states unavailable where it is listed is shown only
- **Decides:** WooPayments' onboarding states 'WooPayments is not supported in the selected business location.' for EG, OM, SA and YE. There its line is shown only, the location its scope. Where it is supported (AE, GB, JP, US), its own service. A suggestion category no held location fills is shown only. (the ruling after WC-5)
- **Why:** the platform's own words say it isn't offered there ([rulings, line 49](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L49)).
- **Risks:** 33 lines name it. Saudi Arabia and Yemen bear on Sharrwah's markets, so a misread changes what the model says for them.
- **Instead:** its own service with the location's exclusion in the scope ('not SA').
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 49](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L49)

#### A line that only one market's page holds takes that market in its scope
- **Decides:** salla.com's plans pages differ by market beyond the currency. The Saudi pages show a yearly discount, savings and struck prices, and nine comparison rows. The UAE pages show none of these, and five rows. A line only the Saudi pages hold takes السعودية in its scope, one only the UAE pages hold الامارات, in the words of Salla's footer. It corrected SA-2's rule 5, which read the pages as one text. (the ruling after SA-3)
- **Why:** the pages state different things per market ([rulings, lines 64 to 68](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L64-L68)).
- **Risks:** 14 lines name it, all Salla's.
- **Instead:** the pages as one text, the differences in the reading.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 64 to 68](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L64-L68)

#### A later answer of the same account governs
- **Decides:** the partner's features of 28 October include `temp-services-redesign` and `temp-influencer-redesign`, which the answer of 8 October lacks. The 20 lines that were shown only behind them in SA-34, SA-35, SA-37 and SA-38 take their capability's left to apps, the feature their scope. (the ruling after SA-44, the second)
- **Why:** the later answer states the account as it is now ([rulings, line 91](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L91)).
- **Risks:** 20 lines' support. Left to apps doesn't count, so no count moves.
- **Instead:** each line by the answer its batch read, the change in the reading.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 91](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L91)

#### A listing condition the platform sets is the entry's scope; one on the store's own data goes in the reading
- **Decides:** a condition the platform sets for a store is the entry's scope, in the source's own identifiers: a named flag, a kind of store, its state (trial, suspended) or a plan. A computed one with no name is named by its module (`use-is-ngo-store`), a reading. A condition on the store's own data (the blog turned on, an app installed) goes in the reading. (M-11)
- **Why:** the platform sets one; the merchant the other ([rulings, lines 23 to 25](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L23-L25)).
- **Risks:** 12 lines name it.
- **Instead:** both kinds in the scope.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 23 to 25](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L23-L25)

#### A conditional title is a statement of its own
- **Decides:** where the platform titles a page differently under a condition, it states the page under each, and the condition is its scope. Zid's charity-store titles (Donations, Donation opportunities, Donors) went to the second stage, for CM-SEL-007. (M-14)
- **Why:** the platform states each title ([rulings, line 26](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L26)).
- **Risks:** 7 lines name it.
- **Instead:** one title, the others in the reading.
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 26](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L26)

#### A help page's plan requirement is its feature's condition, not a plan line
- **Decides:** its plans are the line's scope, and the line keeps its feature's support. A plan line is a plan page's own row or list. Checkout Blocks' features under a 'Plan requirement' note stay its own service, on Plus or 'Basic plan or higher'. (the ruling after SH-30)
- **Why:** the help page states a condition on a feature, not what a plan includes ([rulings, line 150](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L150)).
- **Risks:** 6 lines name it. Read as plan lines, they would be built in.
- **Instead:** a plan requirement is a plan line, built in.
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 150](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L150)

### 4. What is no thing of the class

The new things decided no thing of the class are in the table of group 2.

#### The admin's own frame is no capability's thing
- **Decides:** the admin's loading speed, its navigation's back buttons and breadcrumbs, and its own look are no thing of the class. Its search and saved views stayed new things, since decided as CM-SRF-002. (the ruling after SH-37, the second)
- **Why:** no capability is the admin itself ([rulings, line 122](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L122)).
- **Risks:** about 56 lines name the ruling with these words. If an admin's speed or navigation is a thing merchants choose a platform by, the class lacks it.
- **Instead:** a capability for the admin's own usability.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 122](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L122)

#### A title that names only a grouping, over several capabilities' pages, is no thing of the class
- **Decides:** a portal navigation group 'Others', a menu 'More'. M-13's container line is for a title that names a capability or one part of the platform. (the ruling after SA-28)
- **Why:** the title states no thing ([rulings, line 85](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L85)).
- **Risks:** 64 lines name it. It also decided three new things no thing of the class (Finance, Services, حوّل الزوار إلى مشترين).
- **Instead:** M-13's first rule: such a container becomes a new thing, built in.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 85](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L85)

#### A heading of the platform's public site is its navigation
- **Decides:** M-13 makes a container with no capability a new thing because it is the platform's own admin place, where a merchant, partner or developer works. A heading of the public site, such as salla.com's header and footer, is the site's navigation:
  - where its links are one capability's pages, it takes that capability: الموارد ('resources'), over the help centre, academy, community, blog and updates, is CM-ONB-003;
  - where they are several capabilities' pages (الحلول, القطاعات, عن سلة, أدوات التجار), it is no thing of the class, each link placed on its own;
  - a link to one business sector's marketing page is no thing of the class too.

  (the ruling after SA-2 and SA-5, the first)
- **Why:** the site markets the platform; it isn't a place a merchant works ([rulings, lines 51 to 54](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L51-L54)).
- **Risks:** about 62 lines name the ruling with these words. A sector page could show a capability for that sector, which isn't read.
- **Instead:** read the public site's headings as M-13's containers.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, lines 51 to 54](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L51-L54)

#### A container over several sections of the admin, with no title of its own, is no thing of the class
- **Decides:** Salla's `/tools` and `/more` menus answer with five and six groups (Help Desk, Shipping, Payment, Tools, Logs, Customers). Each page in them is placed by its own entry. A group inside them whose own address is the menu's (the Shipping group at `/more`) keeps M-13 where an entry is about it. (the ruling after SA-22, the first)
- **Why:** like the whole admin's container, they name only a grouping ([rulings, line 83](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L83)).
- **Risks:** about 16 lines name it so.
- **Instead:** M-13: a new thing per menu.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 83](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L83)

#### A container over the whole admin is no thing of the class
- **Decides:** Salla's navigation segments 'main' and 'mega' answer with every section of the dashboard. The class holds no capability that is the admin itself, so the container names none. A container over one part of the admin keeps M-13. (the ruling after SA-21, a limit on M-13)
- **Why:** no capability is the whole admin ([rulings, line 82](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L82)).
- **Risks:** 13 lines name it.
- **Instead:** a new thing for the admin as a whole.
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 82](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L82)

#### The digests' rules that decide no thing of the class of their own
- **Shared risk:** an entry ruled no thing of the class loses its lines from the model.
- **Shared alternative:** shown only, on the nearest capability.
- **Weight:** medium unless the row says otherwise.

| Digest, rule | Decides | Weight |
| --- | --- | --- |
| router, [router digest, line 64](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/admin-router.md?plain=1#L64) (rule 37) | a route is no thing of the class only where it names no place and no thing: no title, path, gate or resource, an id naming only its code role (`cached-web-view`), a catch-all at `*`, or a test page | about 16 lines |
| router, [router digest, line 129](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/admin-router.md?plain=1#L129) (R16.2) | an index route whose component is `NotFound$1`, with no title, path, gate or resource, is no thing of the class | low |
| router, [router digest, line 135](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/admin-router.md?plain=1#L135) (R17.1) | a page whose own words name only a developer's page, with a loader named for employees and no title or store gate, is no thing of the class | low |
| router, [router digest, line 143](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/admin-router.md?plain=1#L143) (R18.1) | a catch-all at `*` that only redirects to a named page is shown only on it; one that throws a 404 or redirects to the root is no thing of the class | low |
| Editions, [Editions digest, line 87](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/editions-page-data.md?plain=1#L87) (rule 57) | an entry is no thing of the class only where it states no feature: decoration, a bare number, an easter egg, a UI string, merchandise such as the Shopify Keyboard | |
| Editions, [Editions digest, line 255](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/editions-page-data.md?plain=1#L255) (after SH-65) | a tool of the platform's company that no merchant or app uses on a store (Tangle, an open-source ML pipeline tool; the Shopify Keyboard) is no thing of the class. Bug Bounty was decided a capability without this rule being applied to it (group 2) | medium |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 60](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L60) (rule 36) | any post that names a feature is never no thing of the class, but for the web admin's frame; the Shopify app's navigation is CM-SRF-001 built in | about 15 lines |
| changelog, [changelog digest, line 169](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L169) (C44.4) | a public page of platform-wide figures is no thing of the class | low |

### 5. What the model reads, and how: the held pass's selection, its second stage, capture as the exception

#### What the held pass reads, and what it leaves
- **Decides:** of the 270 structures the evidence holds for the five platforms, the held pass reads 149 and leaves 121 (`held/selection.tsv`, counted by script). What it leaves, by the reasons the rows give (my grouping of them, a reading):
