  - **for the second stage, by cell:** help pages' titles (Shopify's Help Center contents, Salla's help articles), the App Store's listings one by one, the developer changelog, the status page and the developer guides ([selection.tsv, line 21](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L21); [selection.tsv, line 31](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L31); [selection.tsv, line 138](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L138); [selection.tsv, line 145](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L145));
  - **for the subjects:** market data, such as countries, languages and business types ([selection.tsv, line 70](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L70));
  - **for the architecture plan:** Shopify's engineering blog, open-source code and engineering videos ([selection.tsv, lines 262 to 264](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L262-L264));
  - **vocabularies:** the admin's, checkout's and shopify.com's words in Arabic and English, read through the model's Shopify names ([selection.tsv, lines 269 to 271](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L269-L271));
  - **detail, code and one account's records:** API types and schemas read through their groups, code modules, a form's filters, one partner's apps, one store's menus (for example [selection.tsv, line 67](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L67); [selection.tsv, line 84](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L84); [selection.tsv, line 200](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L200)).
- **Why:** each left structure holds no list of things the platform offers, or another structure or stage reads it (the rows' reasons).
- **Risks:** a capability whose only evidence is in a left structure stays empty until the second stage. The second stage reads only cells holding nothing, so a left structure that would correct a held cell waits for the final review (below).
- **Instead:** read help titles and app listings in the held pass too, at a higher cost.
- **Weight:** medium: it sets which evidence reaches which stage, and nothing is left unread in the end.
- **Source:** [selection.tsv, line 1](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L1); [method report, line 236](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L236)

#### Pricing is read on 21 of Shopify's 61 sites
- **Decides:** Shopify's pricing pages are read on its 18 English-language sites and 3 Arabic ones (ae-ar, eg, sa). That is 1,056 entries; all 61 sites would have given 8,230. Their prices are held for a subject to read as a table.
- **Why:** the coordinator measured that the other 40 state the same lines in their own languages. Every POS page has the same lists, lengths and identifiers. 83 of 84 international pages have one shape. Every plan feature any site names, an English or Arabic site names too ([method report, lines 358 to 360](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L358-L360)). It cut about 7,200 entries, about 650 million to 1 billion tokens ([method report, line 378](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L378)).
- **Risks:** which of the 103 comparison rows each of those 40 sites lists (76 to 101) isn't matched across languages: a gap for the second stage ([selection.tsv, line 36](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L36)). A plan line one country's site alone holds would be missed.
- **Instead:** read all 61 sites.
- **Weight:** medium: a known gap, scoped by country.
- **Source:** [selection.tsv, line 36](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L36); [method report, lines 358 to 360](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L358-L360)

#### Salla's settings centre is rebuilt, one entry per setting
- **Decides:** each settings entry carries what its section's answer states beside it, and a setting held in each store's answer is one entry. That is 583 fewer entries, and none of them needs its section's body read ([method report, line 377](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L377)).
- **Why:** SA-48 cost 455,000 tokens an entry reading those bodies ([method report, line 377](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L377)).
- **Risks:** a setting that differs between stores reads as one entry. The build renders its gates (`nodes:section`, `if:`) ([method report, line 407](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L407)).
- **Instead:** one entry per setting per store.
- **Weight:** low: cost.
- **Source:** [method report, line 377](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L377); [selection.tsv, line 170](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L170)

#### What repeats the model's own record is read as the model's own
- **Decides:** the public pricing page's comparison cells repeat `plan-comparison-public`, the same record the model read. They are read as it (`read-as:`), with the headings of the rows it cites and the page's mobile copy: 197 entries more as the model's own, and 99 rendered row headings ([selection.tsv, line 47](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L47); [method report, line 466](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L466); [method report, line 490](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L490)). The options after the first line of a list the concern map cited whole (`tracking-carriers`, `inventory-adjustment-reasons`, `return-reasons`) are the list's options at the amendment, not new evidence of a support ([amendment notes, lines 70 to 74](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/amendment-notes.md?plain=1#L70-L74)).
- **Why:** the same record, read twice, adds no statement.
- **Risks:** a row the public page states differently from the record would be missed.
- **Instead:** read each as an entry.
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [selection.tsv, line 47](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/selection.tsv?plain=1#L47); [amendment notes, lines 70 to 74](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/amendment-notes.md?plain=1#L70-L74)

#### The Editions are read from each page's data; the changelog whole; versions in their latest words
- **Decides:** the Editions are read from each page's own data, 1,608 features. The rendered text held 2 of summer 2023's 143. Fall 2026 is read from its rendered page. Every merchant changelog post since 2018 is read, 1,646. The CLI and the extension targets are read in their latest words. Kept as they are: the Editions' section descriptions aren't entries, each feature naming its section. A nested entry's doubled parent name ('Basic > Basic') is left, since the fix would change the ids of 222 placed entries. ([method report, lines 356 to 365](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L356-L365))
- **Why:** the page data holds what the rendering drops.
- **Risks:** a thing a section description alone states isn't placed. An extension target's earlier wording isn't read.
- **Instead:** section descriptions as entries; every version of a target.
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [method report, lines 356 to 365](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L356-L365)

#### What a run step or a locale states that no entry holds isn't placed by the held pass
- **Decides:** it goes in the notes, as a lead for the second stage, which reads held pages for the cells that hold nothing. So R5a's scope 'Saudi Arabia' for line 91, read from the Mokafaa page, isn't taken. The Mokafaa and Qitaf rates, Zid's three settings toggles, the feed's named platforms and Zid Insights' trial go to the second stage. BigCommerce's billing period, for CM-PLN-002, and Zid's charity titles, for CM-SEL-007, go there too. (M-12, the join's D6)
- **Why:** the held pass places entries; a page's other statements are the second stage's ([rulings, line 34](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L34); [rulings, lines 36 to 43](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L36-L43)).
- **Risks:** 18 lines name it. A statement that would change a held cell, and not an empty one, waits for the final review.
- **Instead:** place what a run step states, cited at the step.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 34](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L34); [rulings, lines 36 to 43](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L36-L43)

#### Beyond the entry and its source, an agent may read to clarify an entry only
- **Decides:** an agent may read the run steps the ledger joins to an entry, the locales, the model's cells, the source page around a segment, and the answers `index/` names. A reading resting on one cites it at its file, quoted exactly. Scope, terms, filler and quality still come from the entry and its source. (R3 F3; R4 F1 and M3; the join's FX-1)
- **Why:** an entry alone can leave its thing unclear ([rulings, line 33](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L33)).
- **Risks:** a reading leaning on another file can place what the entry itself doesn't state.
- **Instead:** the entry and its source only.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [rulings, line 33](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/review-H-rulings.md?plain=1#L33)

#### The second stage: the strongest passage first, then stop (S2-R1)
- **Decides:** in plain words, as the design states it for your veto, corrected after the second stage's inspection (`inspection-S2.md`, S2I-21): when an agent finds that a platform does have a thing it had nothing for, it cites the passage showing the strongest support first, and among passages of one placement the plainest. It cites a further passage only where it shows a support value, a scope, a quality or a slot filler not cited yet; then it stops.
- **Why:** the count reads built in and its own service, and every further passage costs a read ([S2-held-design.md, line 366](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L366)).
- **Risks:** fewer sources per cell for a later reviewer to cross-check, and a misread first passage has no second beside it ([S2-held-design.md, line 367](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L367)). And the platform's own terms that only a later passage states aren't collected, nor a second source for a limit, a fee, a price or a provider already shown ([inspection-S2.md, line 296](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/38e7501f1c7ef75c1bbc2cd4566678d4f571eb71/studies/class-model/inspection-S2.md?plain=1#L296)).
- **Instead:** cite every passage found, at a higher cost of reading ([S2-held-design.md, line 367](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L367)).
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, lines 366 to 367](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L366-L367); [S2-held-design.md, line 407](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L407)

#### The second stage reads the cells that hold nothing, and the held cells that state no placement (S2-R2)
- **Decides:** in plain words, as the design states it, corrected after the second stage's inspection (`inspection-S2.md`, S2I-21): this stage reads the cells that hold nothing, and the 85 held cells whose statements state no placement; it doesn't search the other 1,130 held cells. A passage met by chance that would correct one of them is noted for the final review, whose selection takes it by risk. Q3's misses are the exception: their passages land on the capability decided, held or not. Its leads that name only cells already held go to the final review's selection, by risk; the stage counts and greps them. (S2-R2; the coordinator's decisions 1 and 7, and its decision 4 on the inspection)
- **Why:** the stage is "each cell still holding nothing" (`PLAN.md`, Stage 4A) ([S2-held-design.md, line 368](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L368)). A held cell whose statements state no placement counts as nothing placed, and a held page could give it a placement that counts, as it could an empty cell ([inspection-S2.md, line 291](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/38e7501f1c7ef75c1bbc2cd4566678d4f571eb71/studies/class-model/inspection-S2.md?plain=1#L291)).
- **Risks:** a correction to one of the 1,130 is found only where an agent meets it while reading another cell, and a noted one the final review's selection doesn't take stays uncorrected ([S2-held-design.md, line 369](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L369)). The leads naming held cells are about 350 each on Shopify and Salla ([S2-held-design.md, line 380](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L380)). The qualities the held pass's selection sends here for held cells, Shopify's compliance reports, aren't read in this stage: a qualities pass, which the coordinator has proposed to you, reads the class's stated qualities from the evidence's side, with the compliance reports, the status page and the legal documents among its sources ([inspection-S2.md, line 294](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/38e7501f1c7ef75c1bbc2cd4566678d4f571eb71/studies/class-model/inspection-S2.md?plain=1#L294); [line 298](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/38e7501f1c7ef75c1bbc2cd4566678d4f571eb71/studies/class-model/inspection-S2.md?plain=1#L298)).
- **Instead:** search every held cell in this stage too, at a higher cost of reading ([S2-held-design.md, line 369](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L369)). Or a short pass per platform after this stage ([S2-held-design.md, line 380](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L380)).
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, lines 368 to 369](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L368-L369); [S2-held-design.md, line 395](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L395); [S2-held-design.md, line 401](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L401)

#### A thing a complete list lacks isn't a cell state of its own
- **Decides:** an `absent` outcome, a complete list that lacks the thing, stays in `s2-outcomes.tsv`. It is written into the cell's reading and `searched`, with its source at its commit, and the cell stays one with nothing held. A not-found outcome is written into the cell's `searched` and reading, as `PLAN.md`'s section 6 asks of a 'searched, not found'. (the coordinator's decisions 2 and 3)
- **Why:** `PLAN.md`'s Stage 4A has the Knowledge Map's page read a cell with nothing held 'as nothing held, never as absent' ([S2-held-design.md, line 396](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L396)).
- **Risks:** the page can't show that a platform's complete list lacks a thing. A reader sees only nothing held.
- **Instead:** `absent` as a cell state, which the merge, the part checker and the page would need ([S2-held-design.md, line 381](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L381)).
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, lines 396 to 397](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L396-L397)

#### Capture stays the exception: a capture request only where the thing could change the model's kind or a decision
- **Decides:** by `could_change`: `kind` and `decision` go to a handoff in `handoffs/`; `count` into the extent report's list of gaps, with no handoff; `no` to neither. (the coordinator's decision 6)
- **Why:** a count changes the model only by one platform, and never its kind ([S2-held-design.md, line 376](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L376)).
- **Risks:** a thing that would move a count by one stays a gap, with no capture to settle it.
- **Instead:** a handoff for every count too.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, lines 371 to 376](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L371-L376); [S2-held-design.md, line 400](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L400)

#### New capabilities made by the second stage get their cells read too (S2-NEW)
- **Decides:** if the second stage makes new capabilities, their cells, searched so far only in the concern map, are read against the held pages before the omission review, as one more part. The held pass's 16 new capabilities are among the 620 cells already. (the coordinator's decision 8)
- **Why:** an amendment part searches each platform's cell in the concern map only ([S2-held-design.md, line 387](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L387)).
- **Risks:** none if it runs; a cost if no new capability comes.
- **Instead:** leave those cells to the omission review.
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, line 387](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L387); [S2-held-design.md, line 402](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L402)

#### Q3's misses are read with the platforms' cells, cut into parts
- **Decides:** Q3's 13 candidate misses are read with the platforms' cells, and the coordinator decides each miss once, from the five platforms' rows side by side. Each miss is cut into parts from its own words, 26 parts on each platform, such as M-01's 'a tip the buyer adds, online or in person; the tip options the merchant sets'. The omission review samples this stage's not-found rows, so its rate of misses is measured. (the coordinator's decisions 5 and 9; the tool ruling of section 3.6)
- **Why:** a miss is a question across the platforms ([S2-held-design.md, line 399](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L399)).
- **Risks:** a part cut too narrowly misses a platform's thing worded otherwise.
- **Instead:** the misses by their own agent ([S2-held-design.md, line 384](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L384)).
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, lines 248 to 264](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L248-L264); [S2-held-design.md, line 399](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L399); [S2-held-design.md, line 403](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L403)

#### Shopify's names take their Arabic by key
- **Decides:** at the amendment, each Shopify name in a capability's definition takes the Arabic its English carries in Shopify's own translations, by key, cited at the commit, beside Salla's and Zid's Arabic. A name with no exact twin, or more than one, is read, not guessed. 225 of its 550 names took one ([STATE.md, line 261](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/STATE.md?plain=1#L261)).
- **Why:** the translations pair 76,267 of the admin's 97,550 Arabic strings with their English by Shopify's own keys ([amendment notes, line 62](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/amendment-notes.md?plain=1#L62)).
- **Risks:** a key's Arabic may be a UI label, not the thing's name.
- **Instead:** only Salla's and Zid's Arabic.
- **Weight:** low: wording.
- **Source:** [amendment notes, line 62](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/shopify/amendment-notes.md?plain=1#L62)

### 6. How the work is run: effort, batches, checks, cost

#### Each batch is checked by the checker and the coordinator, not by an independent review at maximum effort
- **Decides:** after the dry run's review, each batch is checked by the checker and the coordinator. A calibration on a sample replaces the review at `max` ([method report, line 250](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L250); [method report, line 291](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L291)).
- **Why:** a review at `max` cost about five times the batches it reviewed (104 million against 21.7). It was worth it once, to find the brief's faults: more than half the misses were the brief's ([method report, line 250](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L250)).
- **Risks:** a batch's own errors that the checker can't see, and the coordinator doesn't sample, stay to the final review.
- **Instead:** an independent review of each structure's first batch.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [method report, line 250](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L250); [method report, line 291](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L291)

#### The coordinator's check reads the sources only where a cell changes
- **Decides:** the coordinator checks with fewer requests: the checker's lines, the batch's open cases and a measure, and a sample against the sources only where a cell changes ([method report, line 410](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L410)).
- **Why:** at its context each of the coordinator's requests cost about 400,000 tokens ([method report, line 480](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L480)).
- **Risks:** a misplaced line on a cell already held, which changes no cell, isn't read against its source until the final review.
- **Instead:** a fixed sample of every batch against the sources.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [method report, line 410](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L410)

#### Effort stays `high`
- **Decides:** the held pass runs at `high`, as calibrated ([method report, line 293](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L293)). The second stage runs at `high` too, and its first part's check searches again a sample of its not-found rows. If a missed passage turns up, the later parts' not-found rows run at `xhigh` ([S2-held-design.md, line 398](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L398)).
- **Why:** `xhigh` cost 1.6 and 2.4 times as much and missed the same important details, or others like them. Each was a capability whose cell held nothing and that no lead showed ([method report, line 245](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L245)).
- **Risks:** a detail `xhigh` would find is missed.
- **Instead:** `xhigh` from the start for the not-found rows ([S2-held-design.md, line 383](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L383)).
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [method report, line 245](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L245); [method report, line 293](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L293); [S2-held-design.md, line 398](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L398)

#### A structure's later batches keep its first batch's rules, and may run side by side
- **Decides:** each batch follows the one before it on its structure. Once a large structure's first batch is checked, its other batches run in parallel, each keeping to the first batch's rules, and the coordinator aligns them at its checks ([method report, line 292](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L292); [method report, line 380](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L380); [method report, line 523](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L523)).
- **Why:** a run of the same kind costs less: WC-2 followed WC-1 at 55,000 an entry against 76,000 ([method report, line 292](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L292)).
- **Risks:** a first batch's wrong rule spreads to every later batch of its structure until a check finds it.
- **Instead:** each batch from the brief alone.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [method report, line 292](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L292); [method report, line 380](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L380)

#### A digest of the rules in force replaces a structure's batch notes
- **Decides:** the Editions, changelog and router digests replace the batches' notes for their later batches. Where a digest differs from the notes and rulings it draws on, they govern ([method report, line 464](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L464); [method report, line 478](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L478); [Editions digest, line 3](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/editions-page-data.md?plain=1#L3)).
- **Why:** the digests cut the cost an entry: the Editions from 329,000 to 164,000–244,000, the changelog from 189,000 to 152,000–172,000 ([method report, lines 486 to 487](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L486-L487)).
- **Risks:** a rule the digest states loosely is followed loosely by every later batch. The digests' rules are listed in groups 1, 2 and 4.
- **Instead:** each batch reads the notes.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [method report, line 464](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L464); [method report, line 478](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L478)

#### Exact repeats are placed by script
- **Decides:** where a structure's entries repeat pages already placed, exactly, a script places them, its lines checked like a batch's. What it can't match goes to an agent. Examples: the tracking carriers (203 entries), the router's changes between versions, the Tinker catalog, the mobile app's repeats ([method report, lines 451 to 456](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L451-L456); [method report, line 465](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L465); [method report, line 524](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L524)).
- **Why:** the projection's top had reached the 6.0 billion of your band ([method report, line 451](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L451)).
- **Risks:** a repeat that differs in a word the script doesn't compare takes its twin's line.
- **Instead:** an agent for every entry.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [method report, lines 451 to 456](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L451-L456)

#### Every prompt narrows what its agent reads
- **Decides:** each entry carries what its source states, so a section's body or a run page is read only for an entry whose text leaves its capability, support or scope open. Lookups are made together, and context is kept under about 250,000 tokens ([method report, line 406](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L406)).
- **Why:** on the settings centre this halved the cost, from 526,000 an entry to 248,000 ([method report, line 406](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L406)).
- **Risks:** an entry whose text seems clear but isn't goes unread against its page.
- **Instead:** read every entry's page.
- **Weight:** medium.
- **Source:** [method report, line 406](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L406)

#### Batches of about 100 entries, the emptiest platforms first, two agents at once
- **Decides:**
  - batches are cut at about 100 entries, at most 130 ([method report, line 295](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L295));
  - the platforms where cells hold nothing most often run first: Zid, WooCommerce, Salla, then Shopify after your pushes ([method report, line 296](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L296));
  - two agents run at once ([method report, line 300](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L300));
  - a route batch reads an app's bundle once for all its routes ([method report, line 345](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L345));
  - one call prints the earlier batches' lines for the same address ([method report, line 379](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L379));
  - in the second stage, one batch per platform ([S2-held-design.md, line 405](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L405)).
- **Why:** a batch's cost grows faster than its length, since every later request reads each entry's reading again ([method report, line 295](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L295)). Each check so far found something the next batch needed ([method report, line 300](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L300)).
- **Risks:** cost and time only.
- **Instead:** longer batches, or more agents at once, which would shorten the time, not the cost ([method report, line 300](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L300)).
- **Weight:** low.
- **Source:** [method report, lines 295 to 300](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/method-report.md?plain=1#L295-L300)


## Made since the draft (31 October, evening)

These rulings were made after the two drafts were joined. The architecture plan's own three decisions are listed in its section 7 (`architecture/plan.md`), in this same form.

### The qualities, and a step before its subject

#### What a quality is, and a pass that reads the class's qualities from the evidence's side
- **Decides:** a quality is how well, how safely, or within what bounds the platform does what it does, as the platform itself states it: a commitment (uptime, a support response), a bound (a rate limit, a size, a scale), an attestation (a certification, an audit), or how data is kept (where, by which subprocessors, isolated how, encrypted, kept how long). Its kinds are ISO/IEC 25010:2023's characteristics, with isolation between stores, where data lives and identity named. A qualities pass reads them for every platform, guides first, beside the second stage. Prices, fees and plan allowances become the plans' terms, kept by script, not qualities.
- **Why:** you asked what defines a quality and how they were inventoried. They never were, as such: the model recorded them as a by-product of its capabilities, under a definition closed to four kinds, and most of its 3,881 quality rows are prices and plan allowances, with none on how stores are kept apart or on identity. Architecture's first decisions are about exactly those.
- **Risks:** about 75 to 180 million tokens, an assumption. Salla and Zid may not publish some qualities; those stay recorded as not found, and asking them is yours. The ISO characteristics are named from memory, a reading, and decide no row.
- **Instead:** leave the qualities as they are, and let each architecture decision read its qualities from the evidence when it is made: cheaper now, but architecture starts without the qualities its first decisions need.
- **Weight:** high, by what it feeds rather than by the model's counts: architecture's first decisions read these qualities. No count moves.
- **Source:** [PLAN.md, the qualities pass](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/PLAN.md?plain=1#L223-L231); [PLAN.md, its entry of 31 October](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/PLAN.md?plain=1#L665-L672)

#### The cheapest step that keeps a subject's question open may come before the subject runs
- **Decides:** the plan's rule keeps a question open until the subject that covers it has run. It now allows one kind of step first: the cheapest step that keeps that question open, where it closes only what a ruling in force already rules out. Each such step is listed for your veto. Its one use so far: every money amount points to a currency entry, decided before the currency subject (S-09) runs.
- **Why:** the step costs one reference per amount now, and saves a redesign of every money record later. Without the exception, the architecture plan broke its own rule by taking it.
- **Risks:** a subject can find part of its question already settled, and a step's shape may need to change once the subject runs.
- **Instead:** no exception: keep each question fully open until its subject runs, with a review rule meanwhile. That costs a wait and a placeholder in every money record's design.
- **Weight:** high: it lets an architecture decision come before the subject that covers it.
- **Source:** [PLAN.md, its entry of 31 October](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/PLAN.md?plain=1#L674-L677)

### The second stage's other rulings

#### A cell isn't left as not held without the place that would show it
- **Decides:** a cell can be recorded as not held only by naming the place, by its address or its row in a gap register, where the platform would state the thing and which isn't held. A gap entry that names a whole surface, such as Zid's 'other surfaces', isn't enough: the cell is recorded as searched and not found.
- **Why:** otherwise two careful agents split one cell between 'not found' and 'not held', and the capture requests and the omission review's sample follow the split.
- **Risks:** a cell whose page truly isn't held, but can't be named, reads as searched and not found, which looks like more evidence than there is.
- **Instead:** accept a whole surface as the place.
- **Weight:** medium: it decides which cells lead to capture requests.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, line 385](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L385)

#### A passage that may be the thing, but doesn't settle it, is cited and left open
- **Decides:** where a held passage may show the thing but its words don't settle it, the agent cites it, says what would settle it, and the cell stays holding nothing.
- **Why:** nothing gets settled that the evidence didn't settle.
- **Risks:** a real placement waits until someone reads what would settle it.
- **Instead:** let the agent decide on its reading.
- **Weight:** low: it keeps cells open, never moves one.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, line 386](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L386)

#### What a finding could change, and what goes to a capture request
- **Decides:** each row says what it could change: a capability's kind, a decision (named), its count, or nothing (with why), taking the first that applies. Only a 'not held' row can become a capture request. A page held but unreadable has its reader extended instead, and an unclear one comes to me.
- **Why:** capture stays the exception: a request only where a gap could change the model or a decision.
- **Risks:** a gap that changes only one platform's count isn't captured, but is listed among the extent's gaps.
- **Instead:** a capture request for every gap.
- **Weight:** medium: it decides what reaches your local agent.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, line 387](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L387)

#### A cell has one outcome, unless several passages show it
- **Decides:** a cell takes one or more 'found' rows, or exactly one row of another outcome.
- **Why:** a cell's outcome is what the model's reading and the omission review take.
- **Risks:** none of substance; it is a form rule.
- **Instead:** allow mixed outcomes per cell.
- **Weight:** low: form only.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, line 388](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L388)

#### How each of Q3's candidate misses is cut into parts
- **Decides:** each candidate miss is cut into parts, each the subject of its own rows, cut from the miss's own words; three were reworded after the inspection.
- **Why:** a part an agent can answer found or not found is easier to check than a whole miss.
- **Risks:** a cut that is too wide or too narrow asks the wrong question.
- **Instead:** read each miss whole.
- **Weight:** low: how the question is asked.
- **Source:** [S2-held-design.md, line 389](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/S2-held-design.md?plain=1#L389)

### The qualities pass's rulings

#### Where a plan's allowance ends and a quality begins
- **Decides:** a value is one of the plans' terms when it measures money or a quantity the merchant gets with a plan (staff accounts, locations, stores, credits); it is a quality when it measures how the system behaves (requests in a time, a size, a time, a retention window), even when it differs by plan, and its plans become its scope.
- **Why:** the plan says plan allowances are terms, and API limits by plan are what architecture's events and extension points must design for.
- **Risks:** a value that is both, such as a cap on products per plan, is filed by its words, and a reader may file it the other way.
- **Instead:** everything stated on a plan table is a term.
- **Weight:** medium: it labels the model's 3,881 quality rows.
- **Source:** [QL-qualities-design.md, line 353](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/QL-qualities-design.md?plain=1#L353)

#### A quality goes on the narrowest capability it measures, or on the platform as a whole
- **Decides:** where a quality's words cover the whole platform or all of a store's data, it sits on the platform, not on a capability, and no capability is made for it.
- **Why:** the plan puts each quality 'on the capability it bears on', and a certificate of the company bears on no one capability.
- **Risks:** the platform's qualities are read apart from the capabilities, so a design reading one capability can miss them; the page shows them under each kind for that reason.
- **Instead:** a capability of their own for isolation, encryption and certifications, which the model's rule for a capability ('a place of its own') doesn't support.
- **Weight:** medium: it decides where architecture finds each quality.
- **Source:** [QL-qualities-design.md, line 354](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/QL-qualities-design.md?plain=1#L354)

#### A figure the platform reports from the past is filed by what it measures, marked as reported
- **Decides:** a past uptime or a peak the platform says it handled is recorded as a commitment or a bound, marked 'reported', not promised.
- **Why:** the plan's four forms have no place for a figure of the past, and architecture's scale bands need such figures.
- **Risks:** a reader takes a reported figure for a promise; the `basis` column says which it is.
- **Instead:** record only what a platform promises.
- **Weight:** low: it adds figures, marked.
- **Source:** [QL-qualities-design.md, line 355](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/QL-qualities-design.md?plain=1#L355)

#### The plainest passage of the platform's own document, then stop
- **Decides:** for each question, an agent quotes the passage of the platform's own terms, docs or report that states the value most plainly, and adds another only for a different value, scope, date or holder.
- **Why:** every further passage costs a read, and one primary statement settles a quality.
- **Risks:** a second source that would confirm or date the first isn't collected, so a later reviewer has fewer to cross-check.
- **Instead:** cite every passage found.
- **Weight:** low: the work's cost and its cross-checks.
- **Source:** [QL-qualities-design.md, line 356](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/QL-qualities-design.md?plain=1#L356)

#### Engineering writing and marketing pages are recorded as the platform's own account, dated
- **Decides:** they are recorded with their dates and source type, and where the platform's terms or docs state another value, both are recorded and the reading says which is the commitment.
- **Why:** the architecture plan grades a company's blog as that company's own account, and the engineering posts are the only source on how Shopify keeps stores apart.
- **Risks:** an old post describes a design that has since changed; its date is shown beside it.
- **Instead:** leave engineering writing to the architecture plan alone.
- **Weight:** medium: they are the only source on how Shopify keeps stores apart.
- **Source:** [QL-qualities-design.md, line 357](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/QL-qualities-design.md?plain=1#L357)

#### A capture request only where it could change one of architecture's six first decisions
- **Decides:** a quality not held becomes a capture request only where it could change one of architecture's six first decisions; one that would change only a quality scenario is listed among the extent's gaps.
- **Why:** the plan asks for a request only 'where it could change an architecture decision', and keeps capture the exception.
- **Risks:** support and accessibility statements not held stay unrequested, though the founder's quality order ranks user experience second.
- **Instead:** a request for every quality not held.
- **Weight:** medium: it decides what reaches your local agent.
- **Source:** [QL-qualities-design.md, line 358](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/QL-qualities-design.md?plain=1#L358)

#### Who holds a quality: the platform, or the merchant
- **Decides:** where the platform says the merchant or the merchant's host holds a quality, as WooCommerce's hosting does, it is recorded as found, held by the merchant.
- **Why:** the model's rule makes a statement that puts something on the merchant a mention, not a third party's work, and architecture needs to know that the platform promises nothing there.
- **Risks:** a reader counts such a row as the platform's quality; the `holder` column says whose it is.
- **Instead:** record nothing for it.
- **Weight:** low: one column.
- **Source:** [QL-qualities-design.md, line 359](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/QL-qualities-design.md?plain=1#L359)

#### The qualities pass places nothing
- **Decides:** the pass records qualities only. Where a passage would change how a capability is placed, it is noted for the final review.
- **Why:** placements are the held pass's and the second stage's, and one reader per placement keeps the counts consistent.
- **Risks:** a correction it meets waits for the final review, which reads by risk.
- **Instead:** let it add placements too, at the cost of a second check on each.
- **Weight:** low: placements stay with the other steps.
- **Source:** [QL-qualities-design.md, line 360](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/QL-qualities-design.md?plain=1#L360)

#### How the existing rows are labelled terms or qualities, and a third label, other
- **Decides:** the model's existing rows are labelled terms or qualities by stated rules, and the rows the rules can't settle are reviewed once; values that are neither, such as a payout's day or a carrier's delivery time, are labelled 'other' and kept.
- **Why:** the plan asks for the relabelling 'by script and kept', and some rows are neither.
- **Risks:** a rule's pattern mislabels a row; a sample of each rule is checked, and every label can be undone.
- **Instead:** two labels only, with 'other' rows kept as qualities.
- **Weight:** medium: it labels every existing row, by rules that can be undone.
- **Source:** [QL-qualities-design.md, line 361](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/QL-qualities-design.md?plain=1#L361)

### The omission review's rulings

#### The reviewer reads the evidence first, before the model's answer
- **Decides:** the reviewer reads each piece of evidence and says what it states before looking at the model; for a cell the second stage found nothing for, it sees the second stage's search only after its own.
- **Why:** a reviewer shown the model's answer first tends to agree with it.
- **Risks:** more reading per item, and a reviewer who searches with weaker words than the second stage did.
- **Instead:** show the model's lines with each item, at less cost.
- **Weight:** low: the review's quality and cost.
- **Source:** [O-omission-design.md, line 354](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/O-omission-design.md?plain=1#L354)

#### A held cell's missing support goes to the final review, not changed here
- **Decides:** where the review finds that a cell already held should be placed more strongly, it records the passage for the final review, and doesn't change the cell itself.
- **Why:** the second stage's decision 7 does the same, and the final review rules on placements.
- **Risks:** a correction the final review doesn't reach waits.
- **Instead:** land it here, as a finding row on a held cell.
- **Weight:** medium: corrections to held cells wait for the final review.
- **Source:** [O-omission-design.md, line 355](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/O-omission-design.md?plain=1#L355)

#### The walk reads in full only the places its flags mark, and a fifth of the rest
- **Decides:** each place of a platform's top-level lists is read whole only where a flag marks it (no line, only `out`, only `shows`, no word shared with its capability, or one capability taking three or more places), and a fifth of the rest by seed.
- **Why:** reading every place whole repeats the held pass at its cost.
- **Risks:** a forced fit whose words happen to overlap passes unread, at the rate the sampled fifth measures.
- **Instead:** read every place whole.
- **Weight:** medium: it decides which of a platform's places are read in full.
- **Source:** [O-omission-design.md, line 356](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/O-omission-design.md?plain=1#L356)

#### The sample's sizes, and doubling a group's sample where it finds a miss
- **Decides:** section 3.2's sizes, and doubling a stratum's sample where it shows a miss, before deciding whether it is read whole.
- **Why:** about 100 items a group gives a bound near 3%, and more reading goes where misses are.
- **Risks:** a group whose misses cluster in strata of one item each is measured loosely.
- **Instead:** the lean sizes, or a fixed share of every stratum.
- **Weight:** low: the review's cost and its precision.
- **Source:** [O-omission-design.md, line 357](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/O-omission-design.md?plain=1#L357)

#### What counts as already read
- **Decides:** a piece of evidence is read where the concern map, a subject, a held entry or a structure built from it cites it; everything else is in a group to sample.
- **Why:** it is the measure `held_vs_read.py` makes, extended to the held pass.
- **Risks:** a page read through a structure that kept only its title counts as read.
- **Instead:** count as read only what a cell's evidence cites.
- **Weight:** medium: it decides what the review samples.
- **Source:** [O-omission-design.md, line 358](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/29608133787bbf52aca4fb4a0c262af38a117f9c/studies/class-model/briefs/O-omission-design.md?plain=1#L358)

## Appendix: how the three Shopify digests' rules were sorted

*An accounting for the record, not a list for veto: each digest rule that decides a thing of its own is an entry or a table row above, in plain words; the rest apply a ruling above, or say how a line is read or written.*


Each digest gathers the rules in force for one large Shopify structure:
- the admin's route manifest, `admin-router` ([router digest, lines 1 to 3](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/admin-router.md?plain=1#L1-L3));
- the Editions pages' data, `editions-page-data` ([Editions digest, lines 1 to 3](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/editions-page-data.md?plain=1#L1-L3));
- the merchant changelog, `merchant-changelog` ([changelog digest, lines 1 to 3](https://github.com/Hamza-ye/sharrwah-knowledge-map/blob/27ba96d2024dd0b7bbb377a23d79351f5de8c363/studies/class-model/held/rules/merchant-changelog.md?plain=1#L1-L3)).

I read every rule of each. Each rule is one of three:
- **It only applies a ruling listed below.** Named here by its count under that ruling.
- **It decides a support, a capability or a no-thing-of-the-class of its own.** Listed in the tables of groups 1, 2 and 4, one row each.
- **It says how to read, quote, scope or write a line,** by B-extent or the brief, and decides no placement. Named here by number only.

- the router SH-14 to SH-19;
- the changelog SH-42 to SH-46;
- the Editions SH-47 to SH-49 and SH-58 to SH-67.

A reading that names another file's rule of the same number is counted too, so these counts are rough. Lines placed before the digest followed the same rules under their batches' own names.
